1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a waves algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 105. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 779/3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by waves generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 105, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 779. QED.
